### [1] [Quine: Realizing LLM Agents as Native POSIX Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18030)
*Hao Ke*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Quine将LLM Agent直接映射为原生POSIX进程，复用操作系统内核提供的隔离、调度和通信机制，避免了在应用层重复实现这些功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM Agent框架通常在应用层实现隔离、调度和通信机制，但这些功能成熟的操作系统已经提供。作者认为与其引入另一个应用层编排器，不如直接利用操作系统内核已有的成熟机制。

Method: 提出Quine运行时架构，将LLM Agent显式映射为POSIX进程：身份对应PID，接口对应标准流和退出状态，状态对应内存/环境变量/文件系统，生命周期对应fork/exec/exit。单个可执行文件通过递归生成自身新实例来实现该模型。

Result: 通过将Agent抽象建立在OS进程模型之上，Quine直接从内核继承了隔离、组合和资源控制能力，同时自然支持递归委托、通过exec实现上下文更新以及shell原生组合。分析还揭示了POSIX进程模型的局限性：进程提供了健壮的执行基础，但并非完整的认知运行时模型，特别指出需要扩展任务相对世界和可修订时间这两个概念。

Conclusion: Quine证明了将LLM Agent建立在OS进程模型上的可行性，同时揭示了进程语义之外的扩展方向，为未来Agent运行时设计提供了新思路。参考实现已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Current LLM agent frameworks often implement isolation, scheduling, and communication at the application layer, even though these mechanisms are already provided by mature operating systems. Instead of introducing another application-layer orchestrator, this paper presents Quine, a runtime architecture and reference implementation that realizes LLM agents as native POSIX processes. The mapping is explicit: identity is PID, interface is standard streams and exit status, state is memory, environment variables, and filesystem, and lifecycle is fork/exec/exit. A single executable implements this model by recursively spawning fresh instances of itself. By grounding the agent abstraction in the OS process model, Quine inherits isolation, composition, and resource control directly from the kernel, while naturally supporting recursive delegation, context renewal via exec, and shell-native composition. The design also exposes where the POSIX process model stops: processes provide a robust substrate for execution, but not a complete runtime model for cognition. In particular, the analysis points toward two immediate extensions beyond process semantics: task-relative worlds and revisable time. A reference implementation of Quine is publicly available on GitHub.

</details>

### [2] [The Provenance Paradox in Multi-Agent LLM Routing: Delegation Contracts and Attested Identity in LDP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18043)
*Sunil Prakash*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统中，基于不可验证的自我报告质量分数进行任务委托会陷入“溯源悖论”，导致系统性能比随机选择更差；本文通过引入委托合约、身份验证模型和故障语义机制，成功解决了这一问题，实现了接近最优的路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体LLM系统在信任边界间委托任务时，缺乏对不可验证质量声明的治理机制。当代理能够虚报自身质量分数时，基于质量的路由策略会陷入“溯源悖论”，系统性地选择最差的代理，导致性能甚至低于随机选择。

Method: 对LLM委托协议（LDP）进行了扩展，引入了三项核心机制：1）委托合约，通过明确目标、预算和失败策略来限制权限；2）声明-验证身份模型，区分自我报告和经核实的服务质量；3）类型化故障语义，支持自动恢复机制。研究通过10个模拟代理和真实Claude模型进行对照实验，并进行了36种配置的敏感性分析。

Result: 实验表明，基于自声称质量分数的路由性能显著差于随机选择（模拟环境：0.55 vs 0.68；真实模型：8.90 vs 9.30）。而采用验证路由策略后，性能接近最优水平（d = 9.51, p < 0.001）。敏感性分析证实，只要存在不诚实代理，该悖论就会稳定出现。所有扩展均保持向后兼容性，验证开销在亚微秒级别。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了多智能体LLM系统中的关键安全漏洞，并提供了有效的技术解决方案。通过引入委托治理机制，系统管理员可以有效防止恶意代理通过虚报质量来操控任务分配，确保了系统在信任边界环境下的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems delegate tasks across trust boundaries, but current protocols do not govern delegation under unverifiable quality claims. We show that when delegates can inflate self-reported quality scores, quality-based routing produces a provenance paradox: it systematically selects the worst delegates, performing worse than random. We extend the LLM Delegate Protocol (LDP) with delegation contracts that bound authority through explicit objectives, budgets, and failure policies; a claimed-vs-attested identity model that distinguishes self-reported from verified quality; and typed failure semantics enabling automated recovery. In controlled experiments with 10 simulated delegates and validated with real Claude models, routing by self-claimed quality scores performs worse than random selection (simulated: 0.55 vs. 0.68; real models: 8.90 vs. 9.30), while attested routing achieves near-optimal performance (d = 9.51, p < 0.001). Sensitivity analysis across 36 configurations confirms the paradox emerges reliably when dishonest delegates are present. All extensions are backward-compatible with sub-microsecond validation overhead.

</details>


### [3] [A Trace-Based Assurance Framework for Agentic AI Orchestration: Contracts, Testing, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18096)
*Ciprian Paduraru,Petru-Liviu Bouruc,Alin Stefanescu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对Agentic AI系统的保障框架，通过消息-动作轨迹（MAT）和契约机制解决多智能体LLM系统中的长时交互失败、随机决策错误和外部副作用等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Agentic AI中，LLM被用于编排层协调多个智能体并与外部服务交互，现有系统存在非终止、角色漂移、无依据声明传播以及通过不可信上下文/外部通道的攻击等失败模式，这些失败源于长时交互、随机决策和外部副作用，需要系统性的保障机制。

Method: 提出消息-动作轨迹（MAT）形式化执行过程，引入步骤契约和轨迹契约提供机器可检验的判定、违规步骤定位和确定性重放；设计预算受限的对抗样本搜索进行压力测试；在服务、检索和内存边界进行结构化故障注入；将治理作为运行时组件，在语言到动作边界执行能力限制和动作中介（允许、重写、阻止）。

Result: 定义了基于轨迹的评估指标体系，包括任务成功率、终止可靠性、契约合规性、事实性指标、故障遏制率和治理结果分布，支持跨随机种子、模型和编排配置的比较评估，实现了可复现的编排设计对比。

Conclusion: 该框架为多智能体LLM系统的测试与评估提供了通用抽象，能够支持跨编排设计和配置的可复现比较，有助于提升Agentic AI系统的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: In Agentic AI, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in the orchestration layer to coordinate multiple agents and to interact with external services, retrieval components, and shared memory. In this setting, failures are not limited to incorrect final outputs. They also arise from long-horizon interaction, stochastic decisions, and external side effects (such as API calls, database writes, and message sends). Common failures include non-termination, role drift, propagation of unsupported claims, and attacks via untrusted context or external channels.
  This paper presents an assurance framework for such Agentic AI systems. Executions are instrumented as Message-Action Traces (MAT) with explicit step and trace contracts. Contracts provide machine-checkable verdicts, localize the first violating step, and support deterministic replay. The framework includes stress testing, formulated as a budgeted counterexample search over bounded perturbations. It also supports structured fault injection at service, retrieval, and memory boundaries to assess containment under realistic operational faults and degraded conditions. Finally, governance is treated as a runtime component, enforcing per-agent capability limits and action mediation (allow, rewrite, block) at the language-to-action boundary.
  To support comparative evaluations across stochastic seeds, models, and orchestration configurations, the paper defines trace-based metrics for task success, termination reliability, contract compliance, factuality indicators, containment rate, and governance outcome distributions. More broadly, the framework is intended as a common abstraction to support testing and evaluation of multi-agent LLM systems, and to facilitate reproducible comparison across orchestration designs and configurations.

</details>

### [4] [An FPGA-Based SoC Architecture with a RISC-V Controller for Energy-Efficient Temporal-Coding Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18054)
*Mohammad Javad Sekonji,Ali Mahani,Maryam Mirsadeghi,Mahdi Taheri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间编码SNN的紧凑型SoC架构，通过二值化权重、尖峰时间排序器和跳过非信息事件等优化手段，在FPGA上实现了高达16倍的权重内存压缩和显著的延迟降低，同时保持了较高的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）具有高能效和事件驱动特性，非常适合低功耗边缘AI应用。然而，其在FPGA上的硬件实现面临计算量大、内存占用高和灵活性受限等挑战，亟需一种高效的硬件架构来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出了一种紧凑的片上系统（SoC）架构，集成了RISC-V控制器与事件驱动SNN核心。关键技术包括：1) 使用二值化权重替代乘法器，采用按位运算；2) 引入尖峰时间排序器管理活跃尖峰；3) 跳过非信息性事件以减少计算量。该设计完全在Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA上实现。

Result: 该架构实现了高达16倍的权重内存压缩，有效降低了计算开销和延迟。在MNIST数据集上达到97.0%的准确率，在FashionMNIST上达到88.3%的准确率。该设计是一个完全自包含的系统，无需外部存储器。

Conclusion: 该自包含设计为边缘端的实时神经形态推理提供了一个高效、可扩展的平台，成功解决了SNN在FPGA实现中的内存和计算瓶颈问题，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer high energy efficiency and event-driven computation, ideal for low-power edge AI. Their hardware implementation on FPGAs, however, faces challenges due to heavy computation, large memory use, and limited flexibility. This paper proposes a compact System-on-Chip (SoC) architecture for temporal-coding SNNs, integrating a RISC-V controller with an event-driven SNN core. It replaces multipliers with bitwise operations using binarized weights, includes a spike-time sorter for active spikes, and skips noninformative events to reduce computation. The architecture runs fully on a Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA, achieving up to 16x memory reduction for weights and lowering computational overhead and latency, with 97.0% accuracy on MNIST and 88.3% on FashionMNIST. This self-contained design provides an efficient, scalable platform for real-time neuromorphic inference at the edge.

</details>


### [5] [HWE-Bench: Can Language Models Perform Board-level Schematic Designs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18102)
*Weibo Qiu,Yinhao Xiao,Runyu Pan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CircuitWeb评估框架，用于测试大语言模型在板级电路设计方面的能力。通过对300个设计任务的评估发现，目前表现最好的模型总体通过率仅为8.15%，说明LLMs在此领域仍缺乏物理直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在软件工程、数字逻辑生成等任务上表现优异，但在板级电路设计方面的能力尚未得到充分研究。板级设计要求模型同时理解现实物理规律和集成电路数据手册，这是一个极具挑战性的复合任务，因此需要专门的评估框架来衡量LLMs在此领域的能力。

Method: 提出CircuitWeb评估框架，包含从GitHub和OSHWLab等平台收集的300个板级设计任务，覆盖8个应用领域，并配备了包含2,914个真实IC数据手册的知识库。每个任务要求LLM根据功能需求和组件数据手册从零开始生成电路原理图，生成的原理图需通过静态电气规则检查，然后通过电路仿真器验证其动态行为。

Result: 评估结果显示，当前模型虽然具备初步的工程可用性和文档理解能力，但缺乏物理直觉。表现最好的模型总体通过率仅为8.15%，说明现有LLMs在板级电路设计任务上仍有很大提升空间。

Conclusion: 本研究为开发实用的电子设计自动化（EDA）智能体奠定了基础，有望通过在CircuitWeb框架上的持续改进来革新板级设计领域，推动LLMs在硬件设计工程中的实际应用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in various engineering tasks, including software development, digital logic generation, and companion document maintenance. However, their ability to perform board-level circuit design is understudied, as this task requires a synergized understanding of real-world physics and Integrated Circuit (IC) datasheets, the latter comprising detailed specifications for individual components. To address this challenge, we propose \hweb, an evaluation framework that benchmarks the ability of LLMs to perform such designs. It consists of 300 board-level design tasks pulled from open-source and crowdsourcing platforms such as GitHub and OSHWLab, covering 8 application domains, and is complemented with a knowledge base of 2,914 real IC datasheets. For each task, the LLMs are tasked with generating a schematic from scratch, using the provided circuit functional requirements and a set of component datasheets as input. The resulting schematic will be checked against a static electrical rules, and then passed to a circuit simulator to verify its dynamic behavior. Our evaluation show that although current models achieve initial engineering usability and documentation understanding, they lack physical intuition, as the top-performing model achieved an overall pass rate of 8.15\%. We envision that advancements on \hweb\ will pave the way for the development of practical Electronic Design Automation (EDA) agents, revolutionizing the field of board-level design.

</details>


### [6] [A Survey of Neural Network Variational Monte Carlo from a Computing Workload Characterization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18126)
*Zhengze Xiao,Xuanzhe Ding,Yuyang Lou,Lixue Cheng,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对四种代表性的神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（NNVMC）模型进行了工作负载分析与GPU实证表征，发现端到端性能常受限于低强度的逐元素运算和数据移动，并提出了算法-硬件协同设计的优化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NNVMC结合了变分蒙特卡洛与神经网络波函数，虽具备竞争力精度与良好渐进扩展性，但在GPU上的实际部署仍受限于高运行时间和内存成本。由于物理特定的计算阶段（MCMC采样、波函数构建、导数/拉普拉斯评估）导致异构核心行为和显著瓶颈，迫切需要深入理解其工作负载特性。

Method: 选取四种代表性模型，采用统一的分析协议，在模型层面分析运行时间与内存趋势，在核心层面通过家族拆解、算术强度、Roofline定位及硬件利用率计数器进行详细表征。

Result: 端到端性能往往受限于低强度的逐元素运算和数据移动核心；不同模型及阶段的计算/内存平衡差异显著；通过Roofline模型与硬件计数器揭示了具体的性能瓶颈所在。

Conclusion: 针对NNVMC系统的可扩展性，提出了算法-硬件协同设计方向，包括相位感知调度、以内存为中心的优化以及异构加速策略，以推动NNVMC在实际应用中的高效部署。

Abstract: Neural Network Variational Monte Carlo (NNVMC) has emerged as a promising paradigm for solving quantum many-body problems by combining variational Monte Carlo with expressive neural-network wave-function ansätze. Although NNVMC can achieve competitive accuracy with favorable asymptotic scaling, practical deployment remains limited by high runtime and memory cost on modern graphics processing units (GPUs). Compared with language and vision workloads, NNVMC execution is shaped by physics-specific stages, including Markov-Chain Monte Carlo sampling, wave-function construction, and derivative/Laplacian evaluation, which produce heterogeneous kernel behavior and nontrivial bottlenecks. This paper provides a workload-oriented survey and empirical GPU characterization of four representative ansätze: PauliNet, FermiNet, Psiformer, and Orbformer. Using a unified profiling protocol, we analyze model-level runtime and memory trends and kernel-level behavior through family breakdown, arithmetic intensity, roofline positioning, and hardware utilization counters. The results show that end-to-end performance is often constrained by low-intensity elementwise and data-movement kernels, while the compute/memory balance varies substantially across ansätze and stages. Based on these findings, we discuss algorithm--hardware co-design implications for scalable NNVMC systems, including phase-aware scheduling, memory-centric optimization, and heterogeneous acceleration.

</details>


### [7] [WarPGNN: A Parametric Thermal Warpage Analysis Framework with Physics-aware Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18581)
*Haotian Lu,Jincong Lu,Sachin Sachdeva,Sheldon X. -D. Tan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了WarPGNN，一个基于图神经网络（GNN）的高效热翘曲分析框架。该方法将多芯片平面图编码为简化传递闭包图，通过GCN编码器和U-Net解码器预测翘曲分布。相比传统FEM方法，实现了超过205倍（2D）和119766倍（3D）的加速，同时保持高精度，并展现出优秀的跨配置迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于芯粒的系统级封装和异构2.5D/3D集成技术的发展，热致翘曲已成为关键可靠性问题。传统数值方法虽然精度高，但计算成本极高，限制了其在复杂芯粒-封装系统中的可扩展性，亟需一种高效且准确的分析方法。

Method: WarPGNN框架首先将多芯片平面图编码为简化传递闭包图（rTCGs），使用基于图卷积网络（GCN）的编码器提取层次化结构特征，然后通过U-Net风格的解码器从图特征嵌入重构翘曲图。针对翘曲数据分布的长尾模式，开发了物理信息损失函数，并改进了基于图同构网络（GIN）的消息传递编码器，以增强极端情况下的学习性能和图嵌入的表达能力。

Result: 与2D高效FEM方法相比，WarPGNN实现了超过205.91倍的加速；与3D FEM方法COMSOL相比，加速比超过119766.64倍，同时保持可比精度（全尺度归一化RMSE仅1.26%，翘曲值误差2.21%）。与近期DeepONet模型相比，在达到可比预测精度和推理加速的同时，训练时间降低了3.4倍。在未见过的数据集上，WarPGNN表现出卓越的迁移能力，归一化RMSE仅3.69%。

Conclusion: WarPGNN成功地将图神经网络应用于热翘曲分析领域，实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。该方法不仅显著降低了计算成本，还展现出强大的跨配置迁移能力，为快速翘曲感知的平面图探索提供了有效工具，具有重要的工程应用价值。

Abstract: With the advent of system-in-package (SiP) chiplet-based design and heterogeneous 2.5D/3D integration, thermal-induced warpage has become a critical reliability concern. While conventional numerical approaches can deliver highly accurate results, they often incur prohib- itively high computational costs, limiting their scalability for complex chiplet-package systems. In this paper, we present WarPGNN, an ef- ficient and accurate parametric thermal warpage analysis framework powered by Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). By operating directly on graphs constructed from the floorplans, WarPGNN enables fast warpage-aware floorplan exploration and exhibits strong transfer- ability across diverse package configurations. Our method first en- codes multi-die floorplans into reduced Transitive Closure Graphs (rTCGs), then a Graph Convolution Network (GCN)-based encoder extracts hierarchical structural features, followed by a U-Net inspired decoder that reconstructs warpage maps from graph feature embed- dings. Furthermore, to address the long-tailed pattern of warpage data distribution, we developed a physics-informed loss and revised a message-passing encoder based on Graph Isomorphic Network (GIN) that further enhance learning performance for extreme cases and expressiveness of graph embeddings. Numerical results show that WarPGNN achieves more than 205.91x speedup compared with the 2-D efficient FEM-based method and over 119766.64x acceleration with 3-D FEM method COMSOL, respectively, while maintaining comparable accuracy at only 1.26% full-scale normalized RMSE and 2.21% warpage value error. Compared with recent DeepONet-based model, our method achieved comparable prediction accuracy and in- ference speedup with 3.4x lower training time. In addition, WarPGNN demonstrates remarkable transferability on unseen datasets with up to 3.69% normalized RMSE and similar runtime.

</details>


### [8] [Mitigating the Bandwidth Wall via Data-Streaming System-Accelerator Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19057)
*Qunyou Liu,Marina Zapater,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Transformer推理的统一系统-加速器协同设计方法，通过分页流数据流和显式的计算与传输重叠，在PCIe互连和主机内存条件下实现了高达22倍的端到端加速效果，证明了分页流式传输比大容量本地SRAM更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer在NLP和计算机视觉领域取得了革命性进展，但其巨大的计算和内存需求对硬件加速提出了严峻挑战。在实际应用中，端到端吞吐量往往受限于分页数据移动和互连带宽，而非原始的MAC（乘累加运算）数量。现有的加速器设计多关注于片上计算优化，忽视了系统级数据传输瓶颈。

Method: 本文采用协同设计方法，同时优化硬件加速器和系统集成。硬件方面提出了MatrixFlow，这是一个松耦合的16x16脉动阵列加速器，采用页对齐的块矩阵乘法方法（使用4KB分块），配备约20KB的小型片上缓冲区，并通过DMA、计算和DMA输出的流水线调度来高效利用互连带宽。系统方面开发了Gem5-AcceSys，这是gem5全系统模拟器的扩展，用于探索标准互连（如PCIe）和可配置内存层次结构（包括直接内存、直接缓存和设备内存模式，并考虑SMMU/TLB效应）。研究在BERT和ViT等代表性Transformer模型上进行了评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，与纯CPU基准相比实现了高达22倍的端到端加速；与最先进的松耦合和紧耦合加速器相比，实现了5倍至8倍的性能提升。研究还发现，基于PCIe的标准主机内存设计可以达到设备上HBM约80%的性能。更重要的是，分页流式传输和流水线重叠（而非大型本地SRAM）是在现实系统约束下实现高效Transformer推理的最有效手段。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在现实系统约束下，通过分页流式数据流和计算传输重叠的协同设计方法，可以在无需大容量本地SRAM的情况下实现高效的Transformer推理加速。这为未来AI加速器的设计提供了新的思路：应更加关注系统级数据流优化而非单纯依赖片上存储扩容，基于标准PCIe互连的设计也能接近专用HBM的性能水平。

Abstract: Transformers have revolutionized AI in natural language processing and computer vision, but their large computation and memory demands pose major challenges for hardware acceleration. In practice, end-to-end throughput is often limited by paged data movement and interconnect bandwidth rather than raw MAC count. This work proposes a unified system-accelerator co-design approach for transformer inference that jointly optimizes a matrix accelerator and its system integration through paged streaming dataflows and explicit overlap of compute and transfer. On the hardware side, we introduce MatrixFlow, a loosely coupled 16x16 systolic-array accelerator with a page-aligned block matrix multiplication method using 4 KB tiles, a small on-chip buffer of about 20 KB, and a pipelined schedule of DMA, compute, and DMA-out to utilize interconnect bandwidth efficiently. On the system side, we develop Gem5-AcceSys, an extension of the gem5 full-system simulator that explores standard interconnects such as PCIe and configurable memory hierarchies including Direct Memory, Direct Cache, and Device Memory modes with SMMU/TLB effects. We evaluate the co-design using gem5 simulations on representative transformer models including BERT and ViT across multiple data types and system setups. Results show up to 22x end-to-end speedup over a CPU-only baseline and 5x to 8x gains over state-of-the-art loosely and tightly coupled accelerators. We further show that a standard PCIe-based host-memory design can achieve about 80 percent of the performance of on-device HBM. Overall, paged streaming and pipeline overlap, rather than large local SRAMs, are the most effective levers for efficient transformer inference under realistic system constraints.

</details>

### [9] [A Vision-based Framework for Intelligent gNodeB Mobility Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18092)
*Pedro Duarte,André Coelho,Francisco Ribeiro,Filipe B. Teixeira,Luís Pessoa,Manuel Ricardo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VisionRAN框架，通过融合视觉和无线数据，利用深度强化学习控制移动基站位置，成功将视距遮挡时长降低了75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对动态无线环境中移动O-RAN基站面临的视距遮挡问题，现有静态基站架构缺乏环境感知与自主调整能力，亟需一种能够感知环境并智能调整基站位置的新型控制框架。

Method: 提出了包含三个核心组件的视觉驱动框架：1) VisionRAN架构，引入新型服务模型以支持视觉感知数据传输和基站定位控制；2) VisionApp xApp，采用深度Q网络融合视觉与无线数据并控制移动基站位置；3) VisionTwin数字孪生环境，用于训练DQN模型并验证系统性能。

Result: 基于真实视觉数据和仿真无线环境的实验表明，该方法相比静态基站可将视距遮挡持续时间减少高达75%，验证了多模态感知与学习型控制在RAN中集成的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了将视觉感知能力与强化学习控制相结合应用于无线接入网的有效性，为智能移动基站控制提供了可行的技术方案，展示了O-RAN架构支持新型感知驱动应用的前景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a vision-based framework for the intelligent control of mobile Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) base stations (gNBs) operating in dynamic wireless environments. The framework comprises three innovative components. The first is the introduction of novel Service Models (SMs) within a vision-enabled O-RAN architecture, termed VisionRAN. These SMs extend state-of-the-art O-RAN-based architectures by enabling the transmission of vision-based sensing data and gNB positioning control messages. The second is an O-RAN xApp, VisionApp, which fuses vision and radio data, and uses this information to control the position of a mobile gNB, using a Deep Q-Network (DQN). The third is a digital twin environment, VisionTwin, which incorporates vision data and can emulate realistic wireless scenarios; this digital twin was used to train the DQN running in VisionApp and validate the overall system. Experimental results, obtained using real vision data and an emulated radio, demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces the duration of Line-of-Sight (LoS) blockages by up to 75% compared to a static gNB. These findings confirm the viability of integrating multimodal perception and learning-based control within RANs.

</details>


### [10] [RIS-Aided Mobile Network Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18252)
*Adam Samorzewski,Adrian Kliks*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过在波兰波兹南市进行仿真研究，分析了RIS在城市环境中的信号传播特性，结果表明RIS能有效降低路径损耗、改善信号覆盖，并可减少对额外基站的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着5G/6G网络的发展，城市环境中存在大量非视距传播区域，传统基站部署难以保证信号覆盖质量。研究旨在探索RIS技术在复杂城市环境中的应用潜力，以解决高楼密集区的信号覆盖问题，优化网络基础设施成本。

Method: 研究以波兰波兹南市为场景，设计了一个包含八个基站的5G/6G无线接入网，基站采用SISO或MIMO天线技术。通过详细仿真，分析了视距和非视距条件下的多种传播场景，重点考察高楼密集的城市景观对信号传播的影响。

Result: 研究结果表明，RIS在城市移动网络中具有显著应用潜力。合理部署RIS可有效缓解路径损耗，显著改善挑战性城市环境中的信号覆盖，特别是在传统基站部署不足的区域。此外，RIS的应用能够减少对额外基站的需求，从而优化网络成本和基础设施。

Conclusion: RIS技术是未来5G及后续网络中提升城市通信系统效率和鲁棒性的关键技术。本研究为网络规划者和工程师提供了实施RIS技术的宝贵指导，有助于在保证高质量服务交付的同时，优化网络架构和成本。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine the distribution of radio signal propagation within the city of Poznan (Poland) to determine optimal locations for deploying Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS). The study focuses on designing a 5G/6G Radio Access Network (RAN), incorporating eight Base Stations (BSs) that utilize either Single Input Single Output (SISO), or Multiple Input Multiple Output (MIMO) antenna technologies, depending on the network cell configuration. Through detailed simulations and analyses, we explore various propagation scenarios in both Line-of-Sight (LOS) and Non-Line-of-Sight (NLOS) conditions, considering the complex urban landscape characterized by high-rise buildings. The results demonstrate the potential of using RISs in mobile networks to enhance radio signal quality in urban environments through strategic placements. Our findings suggest that RISs can significantly mitigate Path Loss (PL) and improve signal coverage in challenging urban environments, particularly in areas where traditional base station deployment alone would be insufficient. Furthermore, the study highlights the role of RISs in reducing the need for additional base stations, thereby optimizing network costs and infrastructure while maintaining high-quality service delivery. The insights gained from this research provide valuable guidelines for network planners and engineers seeking to implement RIS technology in future 5G and beyond networks, ensuring more efficient and robust urban communication systems.

</details>


### [11] [iSatCR: Graph-Empowered Joint Onboard Computing and Routing for LEO Data Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18539)
*Jiangtao Luo,Bingbing Xu,Shaohua Xia,Yongyi Ran*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了iSatCR，一种分布式的联合星载计算与路由优化方法，通过在轨处理减少数据传输量，有效缓解了低轨卫星星座的星地链路瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星产生的海量对地观测数据传输回地面处理消耗大量在轨带宽，加剧了星地链路瓶颈。现有工作主要集中于原始数据的路由优化，无法应对数据量的激增。随着星载计算能力的提升，在轨数据处理成为可能，可显著降低传输数据量。

Method: 提出iSatTR，一种分布式基于图的方法，联合优化星载计算与路由。设计了利用移位特征聚合和分布式消息传递的新型图嵌入技术来捕获卫星状态，并提出了分布式图深度强化学习算法，在星载存储受限条件下推导联合计算-路由策略，以应对低轨卫星网络的复杂性和动态性。

Result: 大量实验表明，iSatCR优于基准方法，特别是在高负载场景下表现更为突出。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化星载计算与路由，iSatCR能够有效提高传输效率，为解决低轨卫星星座数据传输瓶颈提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Sending massive Earth observation data produced by low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites back to the ground for processing consumes a large amount of on-orbit bandwidth and exacerbates the space-to-ground link bottleneck. Most prior work has concentrated on optimizing the routing of raw data within the constellation, yet cannot cope with the surge in data volume. Recently, advances in onboard computing have made it possible to process data in situ, thus significantly reducing the data volume to be transmitted. In this paper, we present iSatCR, a distributed graph-based approach that jointly optimizes onboard computing and routing to boost transmission efficiency. Within iSatCR, we design a novel graph embedding utilizing shifted feature aggregation and distributed message passing to capture satellite states, and then propose a distributed graph-based deep reinforcement learning algorithm that derives joint computing-routing strategies under constrained on-board storage to handle the complexity and dynamics of LEO networks. Extensive experiments show iSatCR outperforms baselines, particularly under high load.

</details>


### [12] [RUBICONe: Wireless RAFT-Unified Behaviors for Intervehicular Cooperative Operations and Negotiations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18595)
*Zhenghua Hu,Tairan Dan,Zeyu Tao,Jiacheng Qian,Amedeo Morat,Lorenzo Romano,Alessandro Massafra,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: RUBICONe提出了一种基于分布式共识框架的自动驾驶变道决策方法，通过扩展RAFT算法实现多车感知聚合，显著降低了环境干扰和单车误判风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的变道决策如同“渡过卢比孔河”，是不可逆的关键时刻。仅依赖单车感知进行决策如同独自跨越未知河流，具有高风险性和不可靠性，因此需要一种能够聚合多车感知信息的分布式决策机制。

Method: 提出RUBICONe框架，将RAFT共识算法扩展至无线通信场景，利用多个软件定义无线电（SDR）设备作为实验平台，使多辆车能够协同处理并聚合各自的感知数据，实现基于共识的决策制定。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于共识的决策方式显著降低了环境干扰的影响，有效缓解了单车误判的风险，展示了无线通信中共识机制带来的可靠性提升。

Conclusion: RUBICONe确保变道决策具备全面态势感知能力和分布式共识支持，证明了在自动驾驶关键决策场景中引入无线通信共识机制的有效性和可靠性增益。

Abstract: Just as Caesar declared "alea iacta est" (the die is cast) upon crossing the Rubicone river, lane change decisions in autonomous vehicles also represent critical points of no return. RUBICONe addresses this challenge by recognizing that lane change decision-making relying solely on a single vehicle's perception would be as precarious as crossing an unknown river alone. By implementing a distributed consensus framework that extends the RAFT algorithm with wireless connectivity, RUBICONe enables multiple vehicles to collectively process and aggregate their perceptions. Using multiple software-defined radio (SDR) devices as the experimental platform, this study demonstrates how consensus-based decision-making significantly reduces the impact of environmental interference and mitigates the risk of misjudgments by individual vehicles. Just as crossing the Rubicone marked a point of irrevocable action backed by collective intelligence, RUBICONe ensures that lane change decisions are made with comprehensive situational awareness and distributed consensus, showcasing the reliability gain of consensus in wireless communications.

</details>


### [13] [Cross-Layer Traffic Allocation and Contention Window Optimization for Wi-Fi 7 MLO: When DRL Meets LSTM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18602)
*Zhang Liu,Xianbin Wang,Shumin Lian,Lianfen Huang,Liqun Fu,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对Wi-Fi 7多链路操作（MLO）优化难题，提出了一种跨层优化框架，联合优化上层MAC（U-MAC）的流量分配和下层MAC（L-MAC）的初始竞争窗口（ICW）大小，并设计了LSTM-SAC算法以最大化网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi 7标准引入了多链路操作（MLO）以支持多频段并发通信，其双层MAC架构中U-MAC负责流量分配，L-MAC负责独立信道接入。然而，U-MAC与L-MAC之间存在固有耦合，加之无线网络的动态复杂性，使得MLO优化极具挑战性。

Method: 首先，将单链路Bianchi Markov模型扩展为分析框架，刻画网络吞吐量、流量分配与ICW大小之间的关系。基于此框架，构建非凸、非线性跨层优化问题。为高效求解，设计了基于长短期记忆网络（LSTM）的软演员-评论家（SAC）算法，利用LSTM处理Wi-Fi网络中的部分可观测性和非马尔可夫动态特性。

Result: 利用高保真事件驱动Wi-Fi仿真器进行实验，结果表明所提出的LSTM-SAC算法在多种网络设置下均显著优于现有基准方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的跨层优化框架与LSTM-SAC算法有效解决了Wi-Fi 7 MLO中U-MAC与L-MAC的联合优化难题，在复杂动态网络环境下实现了显著的吞吐量性能提升。

Abstract: To support future diverse applications, multi-link operation (MLO) has been introduced in the Wi-Fi 7 standard (IEEE 802.11be) to enable concurrent communication over multiple frequency bands. This new capability relies on a two-tier medium access control (MAC) architecture, where the upper MAC (U-MAC) allocates traffic across links and the lower MAC (L-MAC) performs independent channel access. However, MLO optimization is challenging due to the inherent coupling between the U-MAC and L-MAC, as well as the dynamic and complex nature of wireless networks. To address these challenges, we propose a cross-layer framework that jointly optimizes traffic allocation at the U-MAC layer and initial contention window (ICW) sizes at the L-MAC layer to maximize network throughput. Specifically, we extend the single-link Bianchi Markov model to develop an analytical framework that captures the relationship among network throughput, traffic allocation, and ICW sizes. Based on this framework, we formulate a nonconvex, nonlinear cross-layer optimization problem. To solve it efficiently, we design a long short-term memory-based soft actor-critic (LSTM-SAC) algorithm that leverages LSTM to handle the partial observability and non-Markovian dynamics inherent in Wi-Fi networks. Finally, using a well-developed event-based Wi-Fi simulator, we demonstrate that the proposed LSTM-SAC substantially outperforms existing benchmark solutions across a wide range of network settings.

</details>


### [14] [AutORAN: LLM-driven Natural Language Programming for Agile xApp Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18604)
*Xin Li,Shiming Yu,Leming Shen,Jianing Zhang,Yuanqing Zheng,Yaxiong Xie*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了AutORAN，这是首个基于大语言模型（LLM）的自然语言编程框架，用于实现O-RAN架构中xApp的敏捷开发，能将开发周期从数月缩短至数分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAN系统封闭且单体化，阻碍了创新。尽管O-RAN通过xApps引入了开放性和可编程性，但现有xApp开发过程耗时繁琐（通常需数月人工编码与集成），严重阻碍了新功能的实际部署，亟需降低开发门槛。

Method: 提出了AutORAN框架，构建了一个全自动的xApp生成流水线。该框架集成了多个功能模块，涵盖用户需求引导、AI/ML功能设计与验证，直至xApp的合成与部署，实现了从高层用户意图到可部署应用程序的自动化转换，无需人工编码或测试。

Result: 在代表性xApp任务上的全面评估表明，AutORAN生成的xApp能够达到甚至优于已知最佳的手工基准的性能。同时，该框架将xApp开发周期（从用户意图获取到部署）大幅加速。

Conclusion: AutORAN证明了利用LLM驱动自动化开发在O-RAN领域的可行性，显著降低了xApp的开发门槛并加速了开发周期，从而有效推动了O-RAN生态的技术创新与落地。

Abstract: Traditional RAN systems are closed and monolithic, stifling innovation. The openness and programmability enabled by Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) are envisioned to revolutionize cellular networks with control-plane applications--xApps. The development of xApps (typically by third-party developers), however, remains time-consuming and cumbersome, often requiring months of manual coding and integration, which hinders the roll-out of new functionalities in practice. To lower the barrier of xApp development for both developers and network operators, we present AutORAN, the first LLM-driven natural language programming framework for agile xApps that automates the entire xApp development pipeline. In a nutshell, AutORAN turns high-level user intents into swiftly deployable xApps within minutes, eliminating the need for manual coding or testing. To this end, AutORAN builds a fully automated xApp generation pipeline, which integrates multiple functional modules (from user requirement elicitation, AI/ML function design and validation, to xApp synthesis and deployment). We design, implement, and comprehensively evaluate AutORAN on representative xApp tasks. Results show AutORAN-generated xApps can achieve similar or even better performance than the best known hand-crafted baselines. AutORAN drastically accelerates the xApp development cycle (from user intent elicitation to roll-out), streamlining O-RAN innovation.

</details>


### [15] [Masking Intent, Sustaining Equilibrium: Risk-Aware Potential Game-empowered Two-Stage Mobile Crowdsensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18670)
*Houyi Qi,Minghui Liwang,Kaiwen Tan,Wenyong Wang,Sai Zou,Yiguang Hong,Xianbin Wang,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出iParts框架，通过两阶段服务提供解决移动群智感知中的意图隐私泄露和运行时不确定性问题，在保护隐私的同时实现了良好的效用-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动群智感知研究面临多重挑战：诚实但好奇的平台可通过重复观察和频率分析推断工作者意图/偏好；工作者退出和执行不确定性导致覆盖不稳定和冗余感知；重复的全局在线重优化产生高交互开销并扩大可观察攻击面。

Method: 提出两阶段框架iParts：离线阶段采用个性化局部差分隐私（含记忆/永久随机化）使工作者上报扰动的意图向量，平台基于此构建冗余感知质量模型并进行风险感知预规划，将其建模为精确势博弈；在线阶段通过临时招募势博弈实现轻量级补救，具有有界的交互轮次和低可观察性。

Result: 实验表明iParts实现了良好的隐私-效用-效率权衡，与代表性基线方法相比，提高了社会福利和任务完成率，同时降低了冗余度和通信开销。

Conclusion: iParts框架成功解决了动态移动群智感知中意图隐私保护和风险控制问题，在离线预规划和在线补救两个阶段均表现出优越性能，为未来复杂MCS应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Beyond data collection, future mobile crowdsensing (MCS) in complex applications must satisfy diverse requirements, including reliable task completion, budget and quality constraints, and fluctuating worker availability. Besides raw-data and location privacy, workers' intent/preference traces can be exploited by an honest-but-curious platform, enabling intent inference from repeated observations and frequency profiling. Meanwhile, worker dropouts and execution uncertainty may cause coverage instability and redundant sensing, while repeated global online re-optimization incurs high interaction overhead and enlarges the observable attack surface. To address these issues, we propose iParts, an intent-preserving and risk-controllable two-stage service provisioning framework for dynamic MCS. In the offline stage, workers report perturbed intent vectors via personalized local differential privacy with memorization/permanent randomization, suppressing frequency-based inference while preserving decision utility. Using only perturbed intents, the platform builds a redundancy-aware quality model and performs risk-aware pre-planning under budget, individual rationality, quality-failure risk, and intent-mismatch risk constraints. We formulate offline pre-planning as an exact potential game with expected social welfare as the potential function, ensuring a constrained pure-strategy Nash equilibrium and finite-step convergence under asynchronous feasible improvements. In the online stage, when runtime dynamics cause quality deficits, a temporary-recruitment potential game over idle/standby workers enables lightweight remediation with bounded interaction rounds and low observability. Experiments show that iParts achieves a favorable privacy-utility-efficiency trade-off, improving welfare and task completion while reducing redundancy and communication overhead compared with representative baselines.

</details>


### [16] [Holistic Energy Performance Management: Enablers, Capabilities, and Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18841)
*Meysam Masoudi,Milad Ganjalizadeh,Tahar Zanouda,Pal Frenger*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种特征编排器来协调5G NR网络中的多种节能特征，证明协同优化能显著降低基站能耗且几乎不影响吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络运营商高度重视能耗问题，6G标准也将节能作为关键目标。然而，现有的5G新空口（NR）网络中虽然存在多种节能特征，但孤立地激活它们只能获得次优的节能效果，且往往会牺牲覆盖或延迟等其他关键性能指标（KPI）。

Method: 首先提出了一种紧凑的分类法，将硬件能力与高层特征区分开，并将特征分为两类：一是创建空闲窗口的信令与调度机制，二是利用这些窗口进行节能的特征。在此基础上，提出了一个特征编排器作为逻辑节点，用于协调不同特征之间的交互以最大化节能增益。研究使用了符合3GPP标准的仿真器和产品级真实参数进行验证。

Result: 仿真结果表明，通过协同优化精简NR（lean NR）、调度机制和深度休眠模式，与未协调的方案相比，能够显著降低gNodeB（gNB）的能耗，同时带来的吞吐量损失微乎其微。

Conclusion: 文章最后概述了在可观测性、系统动力学、协调机制以及智能化自动化等方面关于能耗性能管理的开放性问题，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Energy consumption is a significant concern for mobile network operators, and to enable further network energy improvements it is also an important target when developing the emerging 6G standard. In this paper we show that, despite the existence of many energy-saving features in 5G new radio (NR) networks, activating them in isolation yields only suboptimal savings and often compromises other network key performance indicators (KPIs) such as coverage or latency. We first introduce a compact taxonomy that distinguishes hardware capabilities from higher-layer features. Features fall into two classes: (i) signaling and scheduling mechanisms that create idle windows, and (ii) features that utilize those windows to save energy. We then present a feature orchestrator as a logical node to coordinate between features to maximize the gain. Using a 3GPP-aligned simulator with product-realistic parameters, we show that coordinating lean NR, scheduling, and advanced sleep modes significantly reduces gNodeB (gNB) energy consumption with negligible throughput loss, compared to the uncoordinated scenario. We conclude by outlining open issues in observability, system dynamics, coordination, and intelligent automation for energy performance management.

</details>

### [17] [From Servers to Sites: Compositional Power Trace Generation of LLM Inference for Infrastructure Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18383)
*Grant Wilkins,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Ram Rajagopal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对LLM推理负载的数据中心功耗轨迹生成框架，通过学习实测轨迹，能合成新流量条件和配置下的功耗曲线，在多配置下能量误差低于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心功耗模型无法捕捉LLM推理工作负载的特征。LLM推理中GPU会在计算密集的prefill、低功耗decode和空闲状态间快速切换，且设施级需求取决于这些状态在众多设备间的演变和同步，传统模型难以建模。

Method: 提出了一种组合式表示方法，将LLM推理功耗分解为两个组件：由工作负载驱动的运行状态转换，以及各状态内特定于配置的功耗分布。基于此开发了轨迹生成框架，从实测轨迹中学习，并为新的流量条件和服务配置合成功耗曲线，可从GPU服务器聚合到机架、机排和设施级。

Result: 在多种LLM、张量并行设置和GPU代际上，框架对大多数配置实现了中位绝对能量误差低于5%，同时保留了时间自相关结构。

Conclusion: 生成的轨迹支持过载订阅、功率调节和面向公用事业的负载特性分析等下游应用，使基础设施评估能够突破铭牌假设和静态轨迹回放的局限。

Abstract: Datacenter operators and electrical utilities rely on power traces at different spatiotemporal scales. Operators use fine-grained traces for provisioning, facility management, and scheduling, while utilities use site-level load profiles for capacity and interconnection planning. Existing datacenter power models do not capture LLM inference workloads, in which GPUs shift rapidly among compute-intensive prefill, lower-power decode, and idle states, and facility demand depends on how these states evolve and synchronize across many devices. We show that LLM inference power can be represented compositionally through two components: workload-driven transitions among operating states and configuration-specific power distributions within those states. Building on this observation, we develop a trace-generation framework that learns from measured traces and synthesizes power profiles for new traffic conditions and serving configurations. These traces aggregate from GPU servers to rack-, row-, and facility-scale load profiles at the temporal granularity required by the study.
  Across multiple LLMs, tensor-parallel settings, and GPU generations, our framework achieves median absolute energy error below 5% for most configurations while preserving temporal autocorrelation structure. The resulting traces support downstream analyses including oversubscription, power modulation, and utility-facing load characterization, enabling infrastructure evaluations that flat nameplate assumptions and static trace replay cannot support.

</details>


### [18] [High-Performance Portable GPU Primitives for Arbitrary Types and Operators in Julia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18695)
*Emmanuel Pilliat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了KernelForge.jl，一个Julia库，通过双层可移植架构实现了scan、mapreduce和矩阵向量原语，在NVIDIA A40和AMD MI300X上达到了与厂商优化库相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可移植GPU框架（如Kokkos和RAJA）虽然降低了跨架构开发的负担，但相对于厂商优化库在基本并行原语上通常会带来可测量的开销。

Method: 采用双层可移植架构：(1) KernelIntrinsics.jl提供后端无关的抽象，用于warp级shuffle、内存栅栏和向量化内存访问；(2) KernelForge.jl在这些接口之上构建高性能算法。

Result: 在NVIDIA A40上，scan和mapreduce操作达到或超过CUB内核执行时间；在大多数测试配置下，矩阵向量操作达到与cuBLAS相当的吞吐量。在AMD MI300X上也进行了评估。

Conclusion: 证明了可移植的JIT编译抽象可以在不牺牲通用性的情况下达到厂商级别的吞吐量。

Abstract: Portable GPU frameworks such as Kokkos and RAJA reduce the burden of cross-architecture development but typically incur measurable overhead on fundamental parallel primitives relative to vendor-optimized libraries. We present KernelForge.jl, a Julia library that implements scan, mapreduce, and matrix-vector primitives through a two-layer portable architecture: KernelIntrinsics.jl provides backend-agnostic abstractions for warp-level shuffles, memory fences, and vectorized memory access, while KernelForge.jl builds high-performance algorithms exclusively on top of these interfaces. Evaluated on an NVIDIA A40 and an AMD MI300X, KernelForge.jl matches or exceeds CUB kernel execution time on scan and mapreduce on the A40, and matches cuBLAS throughput on matrix-vector operations across most tested configurations-demonstrating, as a proof of concept, that portable JIT-compiled abstractions can achieve vendor-level throughput without sacrificing generality.

</details>


### [19] [Act While Thinking: Accelerating LLM Agents via Pattern-Aware Speculative Tool Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18897)
*Yifan Sui,Han Zhao,Rui Ma,Zhiyuan He,Hao Wang,Jianxun Li,Yuqing Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了PASTE方法，通过模式感知的推测性工具执行来隐藏LLM智能体中的工具调用延迟，将平均任务完成时间减少了48.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体采用严格的串行“LLM-工具”循环执行模式，需在每一步等待外部工具执行完成，这导致了严重的延迟瓶颈，限制了智能体的服务性能。

Method: 提出PASTE（Pattern-Aware Speculative Tool Execution）方法。该方法基于一个核心洞察：尽管智能体请求在语义上具有多样性，但它们表现出稳定的应用级控制流（重复出现的工具调用序列）和可预测的数据依赖关系（工具间的参数传递）。PASTE利用这些特性，通过推测性执行工具来隐藏工具延迟。

Result: 与最先进的基线方法相比，PASTE将平均任务完成时间减少了48.5%，并将工具执行吞吐量提高了1.8倍。

Conclusion: 通过识别并利用智能体工作流中稳定的控制流和数据依赖模式，PASTE有效地解决了串行执行带来的延迟问题，显著提升了LLM智能体的服务性能和效率。

Abstract: LLM-powered agents are emerging as a dominant paradigm for autonomous task solving. Unlike standard inference workloads, agents operate in a strictly serial "LLM-tool" loop, where the LLM must wait for external tool execution at every step. This execution model introduces severe latency bottlenecks. To address this problem, we propose PASTE, a Pattern-Aware Speculative Tool Execution method designed to hide tool latency through speculation. PASTE is based on the insight that although agent requests are semantically diverse, they exhibit stable application level control flows (recurring tool-call sequences) and predictable data dependencies (parameter passing between tools). By exploiting these properties, PASTE improves agent serving performance through speculative tool execution. Experimental results against state of the art baselines show that PASTE reduces average task completion time by 48.5% and improves tool execution throughput by 1.8x.

</details>


### [20] [Literature Study on Operational Data Analytics Frameworks in Large-scale Computing Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19016)
*Shekhar Suman,Xiaoyu Chu,Alexandru Iosup*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于高性能计算（HPC）等大规模计算基础设施运营数据分析（ODA）能力的文献综述研究，分析了现有主流ODA框架，并提出了一种更全面的新型ODA框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据量激增（到2025年每年产生ZB级数据）和大规模计算基础设施（如HPC系统）的复杂性不断演变，系统的可管理性和可持续性成为重要挑战，因此需要细粒度监控和运营数据分析（ODA）能力来优化其效率。

Method: 通过文献研究方法，首先列出支持大规模计算基础设施ODA能力的基本支柱，研究在各种环境（主要是HPC）中运行的流行ODA框架，然后基于Sherif Sak等人提出的大规模图处理分布式生态系统层次结构，扩展Netti等人提出的ODA框架，提出一种更全面的ODA框架，并与现有最先进框架进行对比分析。

Result: 提出了一种更全面的ODA框架，该框架匹配大规模图处理分布式生态系统的各个层次，扩展了现有ODA功能；通过与研究中的最先进框架对比，突出了所提框架的新颖性；同时总结了实施最先进ODA框架后观察到的显著运营效率提升。

Conclusion: 该研究通过提出新颖的全面ODA框架并展示其在运营效率方面的优势，旨在提高该领域的关注度并促进更广泛的研究；同时讨论了该领域的最新研究趋势，为读者提供了该领域的有益见解。

Abstract: By 2025, there are zettabytes of data generated every year. The size and complexity of modern large-scale computing infrastructures like High-Performance Computing (HPC) systems continue to evolve and become complex, leaving us wondering about their manageability and sustainability concerns. Because of this reason, those complex systems are provided with fine-grained monitoring and Operational Data Analytics (ODA) capabilities to optimise their efficiency. In this literature study, we list the fundamental pillars of the large-scale computing infrastructures which enable its ODA capabilities, and conduct a study of the popular ODA frameworks operating in various such environments (predominantly HPC). Based on that, we propose a more holistic ODA framework matching the various layers of a large-scale graph-processing distributed ecosystem proposed by Sherif Sak et al, that extends the ODA functionalities presented in an existing novel ODA framework proposed by Netti et al. We compare the holistic ODA framework proposed by us to some of the state-of-the-art frameworks that we study as part of this literature to highlight the novelty, which would hopefully draw more attention to perform extensive research in this field. As part of creating awareness, we highlight the significant operational efficiencies observed as a result of the implementation of the state-of-the-art ODA frameworks to make the study appear beneficial for the readers, and lastly, discuss the trending research work ongoing in this field.

</details>


### [21] [Why Synchronized Time is a Fiction: Daylight Saving Time, Leap Seconds, and the Guillotine Sharpened for Nothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19099)
*Paul Borrill*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文认为全球同步时间是一种“范畴错误”，相对论禁止绝对同时性，分布式计算中的FITO假设、FLP不可能性和CAP定理都源于这一根本性误解；闰秒等并非对时间的修正，而是对人类约定的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代文明投入大量基础设施来维护同步时间（如闰秒、夏令时、GPS相对论修正等），这些调整都基于一个假设：存在一个全球统一的时间且时钟可以与之同步。本文旨在挑战这一根深蒂固的假设。

Method: 引用Ryle（1949）的“范畴错误”概念，结合狭义与广义相对论禁止绝对同时性的物理事实、单向光速的约定性定义、以及近期关于不定因果序的实验结果，对分布式计算中的FITO假设、Lamport逻辑时钟、FLP不可能结果和CAP定理进行哲学与理论层面的重新审视。

Result: 证明了全球同步时间的假设构成范畴错误：物理上不存在绝对同时性，因果序在某些情况下无法定义；分布式计算中的核心不可能性结果（FLP、CAP）及Lamport逻辑时钟的FITO假设皆源于对时间本质的误解；闰秒和夏令时是对约定的修正而非对时间本身的修正。

Conclusion: 分布式系统理论应放弃对全局同步时间的追求，转而从物理现实出发，承认时间的相对性与因果序的约定性；当前的时间同步机制是在为一个不存在的事物执行同步。

Abstract: Civilization maintains an elaborate infrastructure devoted to the maintenance of synchronized time. Governments mandate daylight saving time. Standards bodies insert leap seconds into Coordinated Universal Time. Engineers debate leap milliseconds and leap nanoseconds. The Global Positioning System applies relativistic corrections at the nanosecond level. All of these adjustments attempt to preserve an assumption: that a single global time exists and that clocks can be made to agree upon it.
  This paper argues that this assumption constitutes a category mistake in the sense of Ryle (1949). We show that special and general relativity prohibit absolute simultaneity, that the one-way speed of light is conventionally defined rather than measured, and that recent experiments on indefinite causal order demonstrate nature admits correlations with no well-defined temporal sequence. We trace the consequences of this category mistake through distributed computing, where it manifests as the Forward-In-Time-Only (FITO) assumption that underlies Lamport's logical clocks (1978), the impossibility results of Fischer-Lynch-Paterson (1985), and the CAP theorem (2000). From this perspective, daylight saving time and leap seconds are not corrections to time but corrections to conventions -- they sharpen the guillotine of synchronization in preparation for executing something that does not exist.

</details>


### [22] [A Pipelined Collaborative Speculative Decoding Framework for Efficient Edge-Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19133)
*Yida Zhang,Zhiyong Gao,Shuaibing Yue,Jie Li,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了PicoSpec，一个用于LLM边缘-云端协同推理的无训练推测解码框架，通过异步流水线设计和稀疏压缩的分离拒绝采样技术，解决了协同场景中的相互等待和通信延迟问题，实现了最高2.9倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统云端服务成本高，而纯设备端推理又受限于资源不足。边缘-云端协同推理虽然能结合两者优势，但如何在有限网络带宽下高效利用并平衡边缘设备与云端的计算能力仍是开放问题，且传统推测解码在协同场景中存在相互等待和通信延迟大的挑战。

Method: 提出了Pipelined Collaborative Speculative Decoding Framework (PicoSpec)，包含两个核心设计：1) 异步流水线，解决边缘协同场景中传统推测解码的相互等待问题，实现边缘端小模型(SLM)和云端大模型(LLM)的并发执行；2) 带稀疏压缩的分离拒绝采样，仅需一次传输压缩词汇表即可完成拒绝采样，显著降低传输词汇分布带来的通信延迟。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法优于基线和现有方法，实现了最高2.9倍的加速效果。

Conclusion: PicoSpec成功实现了边缘-云端协同的LLM推理加速，是一个通用且无需训练的框架，有效解决了协同场景中的流水线效率和通信开销问题，为LLM边缘云端协同推理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements and widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) in both industry and academia have catalyzed significant demand for LLM serving. However, traditional cloud services incur high costs, while on-device inference alone faces challenges due to limited resources. Edge-cloud collaboration emerges as a key research direction to combine the strengths of both paradigms, yet efficiently utilizing limited network bandwidth while fully leveraging and balancing the computational capabilities of edge devices and the cloud remains an open problem. To address these challenges, we propose Pipelined Collaborative Speculative Decoding Framework (PicoSpec), a novel, general-purpose, and training-free speculative decoding framework for LLM edge-cloud collaborative inference. We design an asynchronous pipeline that resolves the mutual waiting problem inherent in vanilla speculative decoding within edge collaboration scenarios, which concurrently executes a Small Language Model (SLM) on the edge device and a LLM in the cloud. Meanwhile, to mitigate the significant communication latency caused by transmitting vocabulary distributions, we introduce separate rejection sampling with sparse compression, which completes the rejection sampling with only a one-time cost of transmitting the compressed vocabulary. Experimental results demonstrate that our solution outperforms baseline and existing methods, achieving up to 2.9 speedup.

</details>

### [23] [DEAF: A Benchmark for Diagnostic Evaluation of Acoustic Faithfulness in Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18048)
*Jiaqi Xiong,Yunjia Qi,Qi Cao,Yu Zheng,Weisheng Xu,Ziteng Wang,Ruofan Liao,Yutong Zhang,Sichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DEAF基准测试，发现Audio MLLMs在标准语音基准上的高表现主要依赖文本信息而非声学信号理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Audio MLLMs在语音基准上表现优异，但尚不清楚这些模型是真正处理声学信号还是依赖基于文本的语义推理，需要系统性地研究这一问题。

Method: 提出DEAF基准（包含2700多个冲突刺激样本，跨越情感韵律、背景声音和说话人身份三个声学维度）；设计可控的多级评估框架，通过逐步增加文本影响（从内容语义冲突到误导性提示及其组合）来分离内容驱动偏差和提示诱导的迎合行为；引入诊断指标量化模型对文本线索的依赖程度。

Result: 对七个Audio MLLMs的评估揭示了一致的文本主导模式：模型对声学变化敏感，但预测主要由文本输入驱动，暴露出标准语音基准高表现与真实声学理解之间存在差距。

Conclusion: Audio MLLMs存在严重的文本主导现象，在标准基准上的高性能掩盖了其声学理解能力的不足，未来需要开发更能评估真实声学处理能力的基准和方法。

Abstract: Recent Audio Multimodal Large Language Models (Audio MLLMs) demonstrate impressive performance on speech benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether these models genuinely process acoustic signals or rely on text-based semantic inference. To systematically study this question, we introduce DEAF (Diagnostic Evaluation of Acoustic Faithfulness), a benchmark of over 2,700 conflict stimuli spanning three acoustic dimensions: emotional prosody, background sounds, and speaker identity. Then, we design a controlled multi-level evaluation framework that progressively increases textual influence, ranging from semantic conflicts in the content to misleading prompts and their combination, allowing us to disentangle content-driven bias from prompt-induced sycophancy. We further introduce diagnostic metrics to quantify model reliance on textual cues over acoustic signals. Our evaluation of seven Audio MLLMs reveals a consistent pattern of text dominance: models are sensitive to acoustic variations, yet predictions are predominantly driven by textual inputs, revealing a gap between high performance on standard speech benchmarks and genuine acoustic understanding.

</details>


### [24] [Continually self-improving AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18073)
*Zitong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三种方法突破AI系统对人类创造者的依赖：通过合成数据提升小样本知识获取效率；利用模型自生成数据进行预训练能力自举；通过测试时搜索探索超越人类设计的学习算法空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型存在三个根本性限制：从小规模专业语料库获取新知识时数据效率低下；训练严重依赖有限的人类生成数据；训练流程受限于人类研究者能发现的算法范围。这些限制阻碍了AI系统的持续自我改进能力。

Method: 提出三项技术方案：1)合成数据方法——对小规模语料库进行多样化和扩增，生成丰富的知识表示；2)自我数据生成——在固定人类数据基础上，模型自生成合成数据以自举预训练能力，无需从指令调优模型蒸馏；3)测试时搜索扩展——在测试阶段对算法空间进行规模化搜索，探索超越人类手动探索范围的算法配置。

Result: 实现了三个突破：模型能够从有限源材料有效更新参数获取知识；证明了模型可通过自生成数据提升基础预训练能力；验证了AI能够在测试时搜索比人类研究者手动探索更大的学习算法配置空间。

Conclusion: 该研究向构建持续自我改进的AI系统迈出重要一步，通过突破数据效率、人类数据依赖和算法设计三方面的限制，为AI系统的自主进化能力奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern language model-based AI systems are remarkably powerful, yet their capabilities remain fundamentally capped by their human creators in three key ways. First, although a model's weights can be updated via fine-tuning, acquiring new knowledge from small, specialized corpora after pretraining remains highly data-inefficient. Second, the training of these systems relies heavily on finite, human-generated data from across history. Third, the pipelines used to train AI models are confined by the algorithms that human researchers can discover and explore. This thesis takes a small step toward overcoming these inherent limitations, presenting three chapters aimed at breaking these dependencies to create continually self-improving AI. First, to overcome this data-efficiency barrier in knowledge acquisition, we propose a synthetic data approach that diversifies and amplifies small corpora into rich knowledge representations, enabling a model to effectively update its parameters from limited source material. Second, to reduce reliance on human data, we show that given a fixed amount of such data, the model can self-generate synthetic data to bootstrap its fundamental pretraining capabilities without distillation from any off-the-shelf, instruction-tuned LM. Finally, to transcend human-engineered training paradigms, we demonstrate that by scaling search during test time over the space of algorithms, AI can search over a larger space of learning algorithm configurations than human researchers can explore manually.

</details>

*Lingavasan Suresh Kumar,Yang Ba,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MemArchitect，一个用于持久化LLM智能体的记忆治理层，通过解耦记忆生命周期管理与模型权重，解决了现有RAG框架中存在的记忆冲突、隐私泄露和“僵尸记忆”等问题，证明了结构化记忆治理对自主系统的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准检索增强生成（RAG）框架将记忆视为被动存储，缺乏解决矛盾、执行隐私控制或防止过时信息（即“僵尸记忆”）污染上下文窗口的机制，这导致了持久化大语言模型（LLM）智能体在记忆管理方面存在关键的治理缺口。

Method: 引入MemArchitect作为一个治理层，将记忆生命周期管理与模型权重解耦。该框架执行显式的、基于规则的策略，具体包括记忆衰减、冲突解决和隐私控制机制。

Result: 实验证明，在智能体环境中，经过治理的记忆系统在性能上始终优于未管理的记忆系统。

Conclusion: 结构化的记忆治理对于构建可靠且安全的自主系统是必要的。

Abstract: Persistent Large Language Model (LLM) agents expose a critical governance gap in memory management. Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) frameworks treat memory as passive storage, lacking mechanisms to resolve contradictions, enforce privacy, or prevent outdated information ("zombie memories") from contaminating the context window.
  We introduce MemArchitect, a governance layer that decouples memory lifecycle management from model weights. MemArchitect enforces explicit, rule-based policies, including memory decay, conflict resolution, and privacy controls.
  We demonstrate that governed memory consistently outperforms unmanaged memory in agentic settings, highlighting the necessity of structured memory governance for reliable and safe autonomous systems.

</details>

*Xin Wei Chia,Swee Liang Wong,Jonathan Pan*

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiTrait框架，利用危机相关特质与子空间引导技术生成具有累积有害行为模式的Dark模型，用于研究人机交互中的心理伤害机制并提出保护措施。

Method: 开发多特质子空间引导框架，结合已建立的危机相关特质和新型子空间引导框架，生成表现出累积有害行为模式的Dark模型。通过单轮和多轮对话评估验证模型的有害交互表现。

Result: Dark模型在评估中持续产生有害交互和结果，成功模拟了累积性的有害行为模式，为研究有害人机交互提供了有效的实验工具。

Conclusion: 利用开发的Dark模型，提出了减少人机交互中有害结果的保护措施，为理解和预防人机交互中的心理风险提供了新方法。

Abstract: Recent incidents have highlighted alarming cases where human-AI interactions led to negative psychological outcomes, including mental health crises and even user harm. As LLMs serve as sources of guidance, emotional support, and even informal therapy, these risks are poised to escalate. However, studying the mechanisms underlying harmful human-AI interactions presents significant methodological challenges, where organic harmful interactions typically develop over sustained engagement, requiring extensive conversational context that are difficult to simulate in controlled settings. To address this gap, we developed a Multi-Trait Subspace Steering (MultiTraitsss) framework that leverages established crisis-associated traits and novel subspace steering framework to generate Dark models that exhibits cumulative harmful behavioral patterns. Single-turn and multi-turn evaluations show that our dark models consistently produce harmful interaction and outcomes. Using our Dark models, we propose protective measure to reduce harmful outcomes in Human-AI interactions.

</details>


### [27] [Reflection in the Dark: Exposing and Escaping the Black Box in Reflective Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18388)
*Shiyan Liu,Qifeng Xia,Qiyun Xia,Yisheng Liu,Xinyu Yu,Rui Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有反思式自动提示优化（APO）方法存在不可解释轨迹和系统失效的问题，提出了VISTA多智能体框架，通过解耦假设生成与重写、引入双层探索-利用机制，成功解决了局部最优问题，在GSM8K上将准确率从13.50%显著恢复至87.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反思式自动提示优化（APO）方法（如GEPA）虽然在迭代优化中能诊断失败案例，但其优化过程仍是黑盒且无标签的，导致优化轨迹不可解释并存在系统性失效。作者通过实验揭示了四个局限性，例如在GSM8K上使用缺陷种子时，GEPA反而使准确率从23.81%下降至13.50%，凸显了对可解释、稳健的APO框架的迫切需求。

Method: 提出了VISTA多智能体APO框架，核心创新包括：(1) 将假设生成与提示重写解耦，支持语义标签化假设；(2) 引入并行小批量验证机制提高效率；(3) 构建双层探索-利用机制，结合随机重启和ε-贪婪采样以逃离局部最优。该设计实现了可解释的优化轨迹与更稳定的搜索过程。

Result: 在GSM8K基准测试中，面对相同的缺陷种子，VISTA成功将准确率从GEPA导致的13.50%恢复至87.57%。在GSM8K和AIME2025的所有测试条件下，VISTA均持续优于现有基线方法，验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VISTA通过架构解耦与双层搜索策略有效解决了现有APO方法的黑盒性与系统失效问题，实现了可解释、高可靠的自动提示优化，为LLM提示工程提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Automatic prompt optimization (APO) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for improving LLM performance without manual prompt engineering. Reflective APO methods such as GEPA iteratively refine prompts by diagnosing failure cases, but the optimization process remains black-box and label-free, leading to uninterpretable trajectories and systematic failure. We identify and empirically demonstrate four limitations: on GSM8K with a defective seed, GEPA degrades accuracy from 23.81% to 13.50%. We propose VISTA, a multi-agent APO framework that decouples hypothesis generation from prompt rewriting, enabling semantically labeled hypotheses, parallel minibatch verification, and interpretable optimization trace. A two-layer explore-exploit mechanism combining random restart and epsilon-greedy sampling further escapes local optima. VISTA recovers accuracy to 87.57% on the same defective seed and consistently outperforms baselines across all conditions on GSM8K and AIME2025.

</details>


### [28] [Adaptive Domain Models: Bayesian Evolution, Warm Rotation, and Principled Training for Geometric and Neuromorphic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18104)
*Houston Haynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于维度类型系统、程序超图和b-posit算术的新型AI训练架构，实现了内存占用接近推理时的两倍、保等级权重更新和精确梯度累积，并引入了贝叶斯蒸馏和无服务中断的温旋转部署机制，从而构建出更小、更精确、可验证正确且可从现有模型初始化的领域专用AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流AI训练基础设施基于IEEE-754算术的反向模式自动微分，导致训练内存开销远大于推理、优化器复杂度高以及训练过程中几何属性的结构性退化，亟需一种新的训练架构来解决这些问题。

Method: 基于三大前期成果构建替代性训练架构：1）维度类型系统与确定性内存管理框架，确保栈合规的梯度分配和精确quire累积；2）程序超图，将几何代数计算中的等级保持作为类型级不变式；3）b-posit 2026标准，使posit算术在传统推理-only硬件上可行。此外引入贝叶斯蒸馏机制提取通用模型的潜在先验结构，以及温旋转操作模式实现无服务中断的模型更新。

Result: 实现了训练内存与深度无关且上界约为推理占用的两倍；权重更新保持几何等级；梯度累积精确；方法统一适用于损失函数优化模型和脉冲时序依赖的神经形态模型；可验证的结构正确性通过PHG证书和签名版本记录形式化。

Conclusion: 该架构产出一类领域专用AI系统，相比通用模型更小且更精确，具有持续适应性，相对其领域的物理结构可验证正确，并可从现有模型初始化，为解决领域专用训练的数据稀缺引导问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Prevailing AI training infrastructure assumes reverse-mode automatic differentiation over IEEE-754 arithmetic. The memory overhead of training relative to inference, optimizer complexity, and structural degradation of geometric properties through training are consequences of this arithmetic substrate. This paper develops an alternative training architecture grounded in three prior results: the Dimensional Type System and Deterministic Memory Management framework [6], which establishes stack-eligible gradient allocation and exact quire accumulation as design-time verifiable properties; the Program Hypergraph [8], which establishes grade preservation through geometric algebra computations as a type-level invariant; and the b-posit 2026 standard [10], which makes posit arithmetic tractable across hardware targets conventionally considered inference-only. Their composition enables depth-independent training memory bounded to approximately twice the inference footprint, grade-preserving weight updates, and exact gradient accumulation, applicable uniformly to loss-function-optimized and spike-timing-dependent neuromorphic models. We introduce Bayesian distillation, a mechanism by which the latent prior structure of a general-purpose model is extracted through the ADM training regime, resolving the data-scarcity bootstrapping problem for domain-specific training. For deployment, we introduce warm rotation, an operational pattern in which an updated model transitions into an active inference pathway without service interruption, with structural correctness formalized through PHG certificates and signed version records. The result is a class of domain-specific AI systems that are smaller and more precise than general-purpose models, continuously adaptive, verifiably correct with respect to the physical structure of their domains, and initializable from existing models.

</details>


### [29] [Reasonably reasoning AI agents can avoid game-theoretic failures in zero-shot, provably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18563)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了具备推理能力的现成AI智能体无需显式后训练即可在重复博弈中零_shot达到纳什均衡策略，并通过理论和实验验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，AI智能体在交互式经济环境中的重复互动往往无法稳定达到战略均衡（如纳什均衡）。虽然后训练方法被提出用于诱导均衡，但在多样化、独立开发的AI模型中统一应用对齐方法并不现实。

Method: 1. 理论证明：定义"合理推理"智能体（能够根据历史观察形成对他人策略的信念并学会最佳响应），证明此类智能体在几乎每条实现路径上的行为都弱接近于后续博弈的纳什均衡。2. 放宽假设：允许阶段收益未知，每个智能体仅观察自己的私有随机收益，证明仍能实现路径上的纳什收敛保证。3. 实验验证：模拟五类博弈场景（从重复囚徒困境到风格化的重复营销推广博弈）。

Result: 理论结果证明了具备推理能力的AI智能体可以自然收敛至纳什均衡策略；实验结果在五个不同博弈场景中验证了该理论，表明AI智能体内在展现出此类推理模式并能达到稳定的均衡行为。

Conclusion: AI智能体天然具备推理模式从而内在地达到稳定均衡行为，这使得在许多现实世界的战略交互中无需通用的对齐程序，为AI在战略环境中的部署提供了新的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed in interactive economic environments characterized by repeated AI-AI interactions. Despite AI agents' advanced capabilities, empirical studies reveal that such interactions often fail to stably induce a strategic equilibrium, such as a Nash equilibrium. Post-training methods have been proposed to induce a strategic equilibrium; however, it remains impractical to uniformly apply an alignment method across diverse, independently developed AI models in strategic settings. In this paper, we provide theoretical and empirical evidence that off-the-shelf reasoning AI agents can achieve Nash-like play zero-shot, without explicit post-training. Specifically, we prove that `reasonably reasoning' agents, i.e., agents capable of forming beliefs about others' strategies from previous observation and learning to best respond to these beliefs, eventually behave along almost every realized play path in a way that is weakly close to a Nash equilibrium of the continuation game. In addition, we relax the common-knowledge payoff assumption by allowing stage payoffs to be unknown and by having each agent observe only its own privately realized stochastic payoffs, and we show that we can still achieve the same on-path Nash convergence guarantee. We then empirically validate the proposed theories by simulating five game scenarios, ranging from a repeated prisoner's dilemma game to stylized repeated marketing promotion games. Our findings suggest that AI agents naturally exhibit such reasoning patterns and therefore attain stable equilibrium behaviors intrinsically, obviating the need for universal alignment procedures in many real-world strategic interactions.

</details>


### [30] [Don't Vibe Code, Do Skele-Code: Interactive No-Code Notebooks for Subject Matter Experts to Build Lower-Cost Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18122)
*Sriram Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Skele-Code是一个面向非技术用户的自然语言与图工作流构建接口，采用代理辅助的代码优先方法，通过仅在代码生成和错误恢复时调用代理来降低令牌成本，并生成模块化、可扩展、可共享的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统工作流执行方式通常令牌成本高昂，且对于缺乏技术背景的用户而言，构建AI代理工作流存在较高的门槛。本文旨在为非技术用户提供一种低成本、易用的工作流构建方案。

Method: 提出Skele-Code接口，支持增量式、交互式笔记本风格开发，将每一步转换为具有所需函数和行为的代码，仅在代码生成和错误恢复阶段调用代理，而非编排或任务执行阶段，采用上下文工程技术优化流程。

Result: 该方法相比多智能体系统执行工作流的方式能够有效降低令牌成本，生成的代码工作流具有模块化、易于扩展和共享的特点，且可作为代理的技能或其他工作流的步骤使用。

Conclusion: Skele-Code成功实现了面向非技术用户的低成本工作流构建，验证了代理辅助、代码优先方法的有效性，为工作流的模块化和复用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Skele-Code is a natural-language and graph-based interface for building workflows with AI agents, designed especially for less or non-technical users. It supports incremental, interactive notebook-style development, and each step is converted to code with a required set of functions and behavior to enable incremental building of workflows. Agents are invoked only for code generation and error recovery, not orchestration or task execution. This agent-supported, but code-first approach to workflows, along with the context-engineering used in Skele-Code, can help reduce token costs compared to the multi-agent system approach to executing workflows. Skele-Code produces modular, easily extensible, and shareable workflows. The generated workflows can also be used as skills by agents, or as steps in other workflows.

</details>


### [31] [I Can't Believe It's Corrupt: Evaluating Corruption in Multi-Agent Governance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18894)

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模多智能体治理模拟实验，证明治理结构比模型本身更能影响AI代理的腐败相关行为，强调机构完整性应作为部署前要求，并建议在赋予AI真实权力前进行压力测试和制度设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型越来越多地被提议作为高风险公共工作流程的自主代理，目前缺乏系统性证据来评估它们在获得授权后是否会遵守机构规则。作者旨在填补这一空白，探讨机构AI的完整性是否应作为部署前要求而非部署后假设。

Method: 采用多智能体治理模拟方法，让AI代理在模拟政府角色中运作，设置不同的权力结构。通过独立基于评分标准的评判者，对28,112个转录片段进行评分，量化违规和滥用结果。同时测试轻量级保障措施的效果。

Result: 在未达到饱和状态的模型中，治理结构对腐败相关结果的影响强于模型身份，不同制度安排和模型-治理配对之间存在显著差异。轻量级保障措施在某些环境下能降低风险，但无法持续防止严重失败。

Conclusion: 制度设计是安全授权的前提条件。在向LLM代理分配真实权力之前，系统应在类似治理约束下进行压力测试，包括可执行规则、可审计日志和对高影响行为的人工监督。机构完整性应成为部署前要求而非部署后假设。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly proposed as autonomous agents for high-stakes public workflows, yet we lack systematic evidence about whether they would follow institutional rules when granted authority. We present evidence that integrity in institutional AI should be treated as a pre-deployment requirement rather than a post-deployment assumption. We evaluate multi-agent governance simulations in which agents occupy formal governmental roles under different authority structures, and we score rule-breaking and abuse outcomes with an independent rubric-based judge across 28,112 transcript segments. While we advance this position, the core contribution is empirical: among models operating below saturation, governance structure is a stronger driver of corruption-related outcomes than model identity, with large differences across regimes and model--governance pairings. Lightweight safeguards can reduce risk in some settings but do not consistently prevent severe failures. These results imply that institutional design is a precondition for safe delegation: before real authority is assigned to LLM agents, systems should undergo stress testing under governance-like constraints with enforceable rules, auditable logs, and human oversight on high-impact actions.

</details>

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了密集人群轨迹预测中的效率和可扩展性难题，提出的聚类方法不仅降低了计算复杂度和资源消耗，还具有良好的兼容性，可无缝集成到现有预测模型中，为公共安全管理提供了一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Crowd trajectory prediction plays a crucial role in public safety and management, where it can help prevent disasters such as stampedes. Our plug-and-play method can be combined with existing trajectory predictors by using our output centroid in place of their pedestrian input. We evaluate our proposed method on several challenging dense crowd scenes. We demonstrated that our approach leads to faster processing and lower memory usage when compared with state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining the accuracy

### [33] [TeachingCoach: A Fine-Tuned Scaffolding Chatbot for Instructional Guidance to Instructors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18189)
*Isabel Molnar,Peiyu Li,Si Chen,Sugana Chawla,James Lang,Ronald Metoyer,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TeachingCoach，一个基于教学法原理的聊天机器人，通过提取教学规则和合成对话数据微调专用语言模型，为高校教师提供实时教学指导，在专家评估中优于GPT-4o基线模型，证明了合成数据驱动的教学聊天机器人的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高校教师缺乏及时且基于教学法的支持，现有的教学指导工具存在局限性：可扩展的教学指导资源有限，现有工具主要依赖通用聊天机器人建议或不可扩展的教学中心人工咨询，无法满足教师专业发展的需求。

Method: 构建了一个以数据为中心的流程（data-centric pipeline）：1）从教育资源中提取教学法规则；2）使用合成对话生成技术；3）基于这些数据微调专用语言模型，使聊天机器人能够引导教师完成问题识别、诊断和策略制定的过程。

Result: 专家评估表明，与GPT-4o mini基线相比，TeachingCoach生成的指导更加清晰、更具反思性和响应性。用户研究揭示了对话深度与交互效率之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 基于教学法原理、合成数据驱动的聊天机器人可以改善教学支持效果，为未来教学聊天机器人系统的设计提供了一种可扩展的设计方法。

Abstract: Higher education instructors often lack timely and pedagogically grounded support, as scalable instructional guidance remains limited and existing tools rely on generic chatbot advice or non-scalable teaching center human-human consultations. We present TeachingCoach, a pedagogically grounded chatbot designed to support instructor professional development through real-time, conversational guidance. TeachingCoach is built on a data-centric pipeline that extracts pedagogical rules from educational resources and uses synthetic dialogue generation to fine-tune a specialized language model that guides instructors through problem identification, diagnosis, and strategy development. Expert evaluations show TeachingCoach produces clearer, more reflective, and more responsive guidance than a GPT-4o mini baseline, while a user study with higher education instructors highlights trade-offs between conversational depth and interaction efficiency. Together, these results demonstrate that pedagogically grounded, synthetic data driven chatbots can improve instructional support and offer a scalable design approach for future instructional chatbot systems.

</details>


### [34] [cuGenOpt: A GPU-Accelerated General-Purpose Metaheuristic Framework for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19163)
*Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了cuGenOpt，一个GPU加速的通用元启发式框架，通过CUDA架构设计、用户自定义接口和Python API，在通用性、性能和易用性三个维度同时取得突破，在多个组合优化问题上性能显著优于通用MIP求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组合优化问题求解方法在通用性、性能和易用性之间存在根本性的权衡困境，无法同时满足这三个维度的需求。

Method: 引擎层采用"一个block演化一个解"的CUDA架构，包含统一编码抽象（排列、二进制、整数）、两级自适应算子选择机制和硬件感知资源管理；扩展层提供用户自定义算子注册接口；易用层通过JIT编译流水线提供纯Python API，并配备LLM建模助手将自然语言转换为可执行代码。

Result: 在T4、V100、A800三种GPU架构上的五组测试中，性能比通用MIP求解器高出数个数量级；在n≤150规模上与专用求解器竞争力相当；TSP-442在30秒内达到4.73%的gap；12种问题类型（5种编码变体）求解至最优；框架级优化将pcb442的gap从36%降至4.73%，VRPTW吞吐量提升75-81%。

Conclusion: cuGenOpt成功打破通用性、性能和易用性三难困境，为组合优化问题提供了一个高性能、易扩展、易使用的GPU加速求解框架。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization problems arise in logistics, scheduling, and resource allocation, yet existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off among generality, performance, and usability. We present cuGenOpt, a GPU-accelerated general-purpose metaheuristic framework that addresses all three dimensions simultaneously.
  At the engine level, cuGenOpt adopts a "one block evolves one solution" CUDA architecture with a unified encoding abstraction (permutation, binary, integer), a two-level adaptive operator selection mechanism, and hardware-aware resource management. At the extensibility level, a user-defined operator registration interface allows domain experts to inject problem-specific CUDA search operators. At the usability level, a JIT compilation pipeline exposes the framework as a pure-Python API, and an LLM-based modeling assistant converts natural-language problem descriptions into executable solver code.
  Experiments across five thematic suites on three GPU architectures (T4, V100, A800) show that cuGenOpt outperforms general MIP solvers by orders of magnitude, achieves competitive quality against specialized solvers on instances up to n=150, and attains 4.73% gap on TSP-442 within 30s. Twelve problem types spanning five encoding variants are solved to optimality. Framework-level optimizations cumulatively reduce pcb442 gap from 36% to 4.73% and boost VRPTW throughput by 75-81%.
  Code: https://github.com/L-yang-yang/cugenopt

</details>
